#upgrade palace
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit	
			value = 1
			}
		}
			}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 21
			}
			}
		}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 41
			}
		}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1D_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 61
			}
		}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1E_eventPicture
	}

		}
	}

	}

			}
	}	
	}

	}

	trigger = {
	NOT = {

			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 121
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1H_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 141
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1B_eventPicture
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}	


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_1	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_1_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_2	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_2_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_3	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_3_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_4	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_4_upgrade
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_5	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_5_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_6	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_6_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_7	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_7_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_8	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_8_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}
		}								
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_1_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_2_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_3_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_4_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_5_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_6_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_7_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_8_upgrade	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_all_upgrade
		}
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_1
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_2
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_3
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_4
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_5
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_6
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_7
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_8						
		}					
}

#Start event from estate
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.10
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.10.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.10.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_6B_1_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
		has_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
	}
	has_ruler_flag = cosmo_wives_launch
			OR = {
				culture_group = eskaleut		
				culture_group = central_algonquian		
				culture_group = eastern_algonquian		
				culture_group = iroquoian
				culture_group = siouan
				culture_group = plains_algonquian		
				culture_group = caddoan	
				culture_group = muskogean		
				culture_group = sonoran		
				culture_group = apachean			
				culture_group = na_dene		
				culture_group = penutian		
			}		
		}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_wives_launch
		remove_estate_privilege= estate_wives_ruler_visit
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			}
			random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_mil
	
				}																				
			}
		}		
		}
		}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.10.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.30
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}	
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.10100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_isolation_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_advisors_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_onsenaffairs_events.401
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_renovate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1
			}
		}																										
	}					
}

#grant wives 1a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.11
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.11.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.11.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_1A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_1a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = anishinabe
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = anishinabe
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = anishinabe
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_1a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = na_concubine_1a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = anishinabe
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = anishinabe
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = anishinabe
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = na_concubine_1a
				duration =  -1
				desc = na_concubine_1a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.11.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = anishinabe
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = anishinabe
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_nawives_1a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_namarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.11.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}						
		}					
}

#grant wives 1c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.13
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.13.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.13.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_1C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_1c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 20
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = huron
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 20
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = huron
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = huron
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_1c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = na_concubine_1c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = huron
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = huron
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = huron
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = na_concubine_1c
				duration =  -1
				desc = na_concubine_1c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.13.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = huron
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = huron
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_nawives_1c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_namarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.13.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem

			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = mikmaq
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = mikmaq
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = mikmaq
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_2c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = na_concubine_2c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = mikmaq
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = mikmaq
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = mikmaq
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = na_concubine_2c
				duration =  -1
				desc = na_concubine_2c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}

			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

			duration = 3650
		}

#grant wives 4a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.41
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.41.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.41.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_4A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_4a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = chiwere
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = chiwere
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = chiwere
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_4a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = na_concubine_4a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = chiwere
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = chiwere
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = chiwere
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = na_concubine_4a
				duration =  -1
				desc = na_concubine_4a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}

#grant wives 5a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.51
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.51.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.51.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_5A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_5a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = pequot
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = pequot
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = pequot
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_5a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = na_concubine_5a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = pequot
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = pequot
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = pequot
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = na_concubine_5a
				duration =  -1
				desc = na_concubine_5a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.51.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = pequot
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = pequot
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_nawives_5a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_namarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.51.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10

		}

	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.61.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.61.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_6A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_6a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = choctaw
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = choctaw
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = choctaw
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_6a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = na_concubine_6a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = choctaw
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = choctaw
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = choctaw
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = na_concubine_6a
				duration =  -1
				desc = na_concubine_6a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.61.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = choctaw
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = choctaw
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_nawives_6a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_namarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.61.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}						
		}					
}

#grant wives 7b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.72
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.72.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.72.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_7B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_7b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = caddo
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = caddo
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = caddo
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_7b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = na_concubine_7b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = caddo
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = caddo
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = caddo
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650

			influence = -10

		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10

		}

			add_province_modifier = {

				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = na_concubine_7b

#grant wives 8b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.82
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.82.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.82.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_8B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_8b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = dakota
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = dakota
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = dakota
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_8b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = na_concubine_8b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = dakota
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = dakota
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = dakota
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = na_concubine_8b
				duration =  -1
				desc = na_concubine_8b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.82.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = dakota
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = dakota
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_nawives_8b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_namarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.82.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

#grant wives 9b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.92
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.92.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.92.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_9B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_9b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = cheyenne
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = cheyenne
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = cheyenne
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_9b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = na_concubine_9b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = cheyenne
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = cheyenne
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = cheyenne
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = na_concubine_9b
				duration =  -1
				desc = na_concubine_9b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.92.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = cheyenne
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = cheyenne
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_nawives_9b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_namarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.92.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA

		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = salish
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = salish

			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}

				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1003
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1003.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1003.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_10C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_10c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = inuit
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = inuit
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = inuit
				}
		}
			
	}

#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_10c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = na_concubine_10c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = inuit
			}

				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = shoshone
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = shoshone
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_nawives_11b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_namarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}

#grant wives 11c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1013
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1013.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1013.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_11C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_11c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = yokuts
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = yokuts
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = yokuts
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_11c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = na_concubine_11c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = yokuts
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = yokuts
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = yokuts
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = na_concubine_11c
				duration =  -1
				desc = na_concubine_11c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1013.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs

#grant wives 12c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1023
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1023.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1023.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_12C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_12c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = pima
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = pima
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = pima
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_12c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = na_concubine_12c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = pima
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = pima
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = pima
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = na_concubine_12c
				duration =  -1
				desc = na_concubine_12c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1023.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = pima
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = pima
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_nawives_12c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_namarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1023.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10

		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.101
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.101.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.101.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_1_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_1_upgrade
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_all_upgrade
			}
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_1a
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_1b
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_1c
			}
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_2_upgrade
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_all_upgrade
			}
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_2a
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_2b

			}
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_3_upgrade

		if = {

				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_all_upgrade
			}

			}
		}	
		}

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_5_upgrade
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_all_upgrade
			}
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_5a
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_5b

			}
		}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.10100
	title = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.10100.t
	desc = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.10100.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_NA_MENU_1_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_1_upgrade
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_all_upgrade
			}
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_1a
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_1b
				has_province_modifier = na_concubine_1c
			}
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_2_upgrade
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_menu_all_upgrade
			}

			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_eurowese_diplomat
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesOneCCounter	
			value = 281
			}
			}	
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesOneCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}	
}

						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16c
					}
				}				
			}																
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.101
			}
			}																					
		}				
}

		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.430.a	   
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }

		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoKoreanCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesFourCCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesFourCCounter	
			value = 460
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesFourCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

			id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.101
			}
		}

			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

		}		
		}

		}

		name = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.600.b

				}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16c
					}
				}				
			}																
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.101
			}
			}																					
		}			
}

			}

		}

			which = cosmoNAWivesTwelveACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesTwelveACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15c
					}
				}								
			}					
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16c
					}
				}
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1600
			}
			}			
			if = {
				limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_14c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16c
					}
				}

		value = 20
		}

		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesThirteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesThirteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesThirteenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15c
					}
				}
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1500
			}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15c
					}
				}								
			}					
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16c
					}
				}
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.1600
			}
			}			
			if = {
				limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_15c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16a

		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesFourteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesFourteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesFourteenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16a
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16b
						has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16c
					}
				}				
			}																
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.101
			}
			}																								
		}

				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoNACounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesFifteenACounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesFifteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoNAWivesFifteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoNAWivesFifteenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

country_event = {

	}

		}
		}

			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16a
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt

			id = cosmo_nawivesestate_events.101
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {

			has_province_modifier = na_concubine_16a
		}		
		}
			country_event = {

		}
		}

		if = {

		}
